# ClinicalTrials.gov record NCT01730534

## Identification
- Acronym: DECLARE-TIMI58
- Brief title: Multicenter Trial to Evaluate the Effect of Dapagliflozin on the Incidence of Cardiovascular Events
- Nct id: NCT01730534
- Official title: Dapagliflozin Effect on Cardiovascular Events A Multicenter, Randomized, Double-Blind, Placebo-Controlled Trial to Evaluate the Effect of Dapagliflozin 10 mg Once Daily on the Incidence of Cardiovascular Death, Myocardial Infarction or Ischemic Stroke in Patients With Type 2 Diabetes

### Org study id info
- Id: D1693C00001

### Organization
- Class: INDUSTRY
- Full name: AstraZeneca

- Completion date: 2018-09-11 (ACTUAL)

## Oversight
- Oversight has dmc: true

## Description
- Brief summary: This study is being carried out to determine the effect of dapagliflozin on cardiovacular outcomes when added to current background therapy in patients with type 2 diabetes with either established cardiovacular disease or cardiovascular risk factors.

## Conditions
- Conditions: Diabetes Mellitus, Non-Insulin-Dependent; High Risk for Cardiovascular Event
- Keywords: Phase IIIb; T2DM; cardiovascular events

## Design
- Phases: PHASE3
- Study type: INTERVENTIONAL

### Design info
- Allocation: RANDOMIZED
- Intervention model: PARALLEL
- Primary purpose: TREATMENT

#### Masking info
- Masking: QUADRUPLE
- Who masked: PARTICIPANT; CARE_PROVIDER; INVESTIGATOR; OUTCOMES_ASSESSOR

### Enrollment info
- Count: 17190
- Type: ACTUAL

## Arms and interventions

### Arm groups 1: Dapagliflozin
- Description: Dapagliflozin + standard of care therapy for Type 2 Diabetes and for co-morbidities and cardiovascular risk factors
- Intervention names: Drug: Dapagliflozin 10 mg
- Label: Dapagliflozin
- Type: EXPERIMENTAL

### Arm groups 2: Placebo
- Description: Placebo + standard of care therapy for Type 2 Diabetes and for co-morbidities and cardiovascular risk factors
- Intervention names: Drug: Placebo tablet
- Label: Placebo
- Type: PLACEBO_COMPARATOR

### Interventions 1: Dapagliflozin 10 mg
- Arm group labels: Dapagliflozin
- Description: Oral dose (od)
- Name: Dapagliflozin 10 mg
- Type: DRUG

### Interventions 2: Placebo tablet
- Arm group labels: Placebo
- Description: Oral dose (od)
- Name: Placebo tablet
- Type: DRUG

## Registered outcomes

### Primary outcome 1: Subjects Included in the Composite Endpoint of CV Death, MI or Ischemic Stroke
- Description: Safety and co-primary efficacy
- Measure: Subjects Included in the Composite Endpoint of CV Death, MI or Ischemic Stroke
- Time frame: up to 5.2 years

### Primary outcome 2: Subjects Included in the Composite Endpoint of CV Death or Hospitalization Due to Heart Failure.
- Description: Co-primary efficacy
- Measure: Subjects Included in the Composite Endpoint of CV Death or Hospitalization Due to Heart Failure.
- Time frame: up to 5.2 years

### Secondary outcome 1: Subjects Included in the Renal Composite Endpoint: Confirmed Sustained ≥40% Decrease in eGFR to eGFR <60 ml/Min/1.73m2 and/or ESRD and/or Renal or CV Death.
- Description: Secondary
- Measure: Subjects Included in the Renal Composite Endpoint: Confirmed Sustained ≥40% Decrease in eGFR to eGFR <60 ml/Min/1.73m2 and/or ESRD and/or Renal or CV Death.
- Time frame: up to 5.2 years

### Secondary outcome 2: Subjects Included in the Endpoint of All-cause Mortality.
- Description: Secondary
- Measure: Subjects Included in the Endpoint of All-cause Mortality.
- Time frame: up to 5.2 years

## Eligibility
- Eligibility criteria: Inclusion Criteria:

* Provision of informed consent prior to any study specific procedures
* Female or male aged ≥40 years
* Diagnosed with Type 2 Diabetes
* High Risk for Cardiovascular events

Exclusion Criteria:

* Diagnosis of Type 1 diabetes mellitus History of bladder cancer or history of radiation therapy to the lower abdomen or pelvis at any time
* Chronic cystitis and/or recurrent urinary tract infections
* Pregnant or breast-feeding patients
- Healthy volunteers: false
- Maximum age: 130 Years
- Minimum age: 40 Years
- Sex: ALL
- Std ages: ADULT; OLDER_ADULT

## Results: participant flow

Groups:
- FG000 Dapa 10 mg: Dapagliflozin 10 mg tablets administered orally once daily
- FG001 Placebo: Matching placebo for dapagliflozin 10 mg administered orally once daily

### Period: Overall Study

|---|---|---|
| STARTED | 8582 | 8578 |
| COMPLETED | 8473 | 8433 |
| NOT COMPLETED | 109 | 145 |

Reasons for not completing:

| | Dapa 10 mg | Placebo |
|---|---|---|
| Withdrawal by Subject | 97 | 127 |
| Lost to Follow-up | 12 | 18 |

## Results: outcome measures

### Outcome measure 1: Subjects Included in the Composite Endpoint of CV Death, MI or Ischemic Stroke
- Description: Safety and co-primary efficacy
- Param type: COUNT_OF_PARTICIPANTS
- Reporting status: POSTED
- Time frame: up to 5.2 years
- Type: PRIMARY
- Unit of measure: Participants

Groups:
- OG000 Dapa 10 mg
- OG001 Placebo

| | Dapa 10 mg | Placebo |
|---|---|---|
| Analysed (Participants) | 8582 | 8578 |
| Number of patients with an event | 756 | 803 |

#### Statistical analysis 1
- Ci lower limit: 0.84
- Ci pct value: 95
- Ci upper limit: 1.03
- Group ids: OG000; OG001
- Non inferiority type: SUPERIORITY
- P value: 0.172
- Param type: Hazard Ratio (HR)
- Param value: 0.93
- Statistical method: Regression, Cox

## Results: adverse events
- Description: All-Cause Mortality was assessed for all participants. Serious and Other Adverse Events were only assessed for the Safety Analysis Population, which included participants that received at least one dose of study drug and who have data observed at any time after first randomized dose until the end of the study.
- Frequency threshold: 1
- Time frame: up to 5.2 years

Groups:
- EG000 Dapa 10 mg: Description (Arm-group)
- EG001 Placebo

| | Dapa 10 mg | Placebo |
|---|---|---|
| Deaths num affected | 529 | 570 |
| Deaths num at risk | 8582 | 8578 |
| Other num affected | 268 | 342 |
| Other num at risk | 8574 | 8569 |
| Serious num affected | 3205 | 3418 |
| Serious num at risk | 8574 | 8569 |

### Serious adverse events (affected / at risk)

| | Dapa 10 mg | Placebo |
|---|---|---|
